#### Audit-batch PR dispositions (2026-07-02)

**#390 was closed with disposition** and re-cut fresh as P23 (`#431`, then `#432`). The owner later directed the remaining `main.py` refactor to be treated as complete for this audit.

### Execution design — wave model

Each wave is a theme for scheduling; each tracker row is an independent PR. A later wave can begin only when it does not conflict with active uncommitted work in the same repo.

- **Wave 0 — tracking and truth sync:** the docs-only bootstrap PR (#423) + this consolidation.
- **Wave 1 — engine cost/correctness:** paid re-bill, remote-source egress, ffmpeg timeouts, failed mypy fix. ✅ complete (P1–P4).
- **Wave 2 — engine resource durability:** temp scratch lifecycle, GDrive API streaming/safe put, Cloud Run timeout handling, upload retention scheduling. ✅ complete (P5-P8).
- **Wave 3 — supply-chain and CI gates:** SHA pins, obsreport CI, sports-data-collector ruff/coverage, khelsutra E2E CUJ, collector artifact policy. ✅ complete (P9-P14).
- **Wave 4 — UX/UI resilience:** vendored SPA bundle, job resume/progress, upload filename, demo-grid hiding, HTML 5xx handling, WhatsApp file share. ✅ complete (P15-P22).
- **Wave 5 — structural debt:** re-cut `#390`, apply owner-approved `main.py` split steps, scope-retire remaining `main.py` extraction, then split database facade. ✅ complete (P23-P25).
- **Wave 6 — issue hygiene and archival:** issue hygiene, Roora non-video archive, owned-source MD5 metadata. ✅ complete (P26-P28).

### Verification policy

For every code PR:

- Run the repo's normal CI-equivalent checks locally when feasible, or cite the green CI rollup and run only local checks that CI cannot cover, matching the repo's review policy.
- Preserve or raise coverage floors. Never lower a coverage floor without owner approval.
- Install missing test dependencies if the suite otherwise fails to collect; record unavoidable private/GPU/real-video skips.
- For engine PRs, default target is `python run_all_tests.py --cov=backend --cov=training --cov-report=term --cov-fail-under=70`, `ruff check .`, and `mypy backend training`, adjusted only for documented CI-policy exceptions.
- For `khelsutra/web` PRs, run typecheck, coverage, build, and any relevant Playwright CUJ; create/validate screencast evidence for UI behavior.
- For visual/UI PRs, attach the screencast or screenshots in the PR body and store durable artifacts under the existing `khelsutra-screencast/` workflow when the repo's harness supports it.

### Risk table

| Risk | Where it bites | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| Shared checkout absorbs sibling changes | All repos | Fetch/branch from `origin/<base>`, re-verify branch + HEAD before staging, stage explicit paths. |
| Docs-only PR accidentally includes local WIP | Especially collector `.gitignore` | Docs PRs touch docs only; the collector PR stages explicit paths and handles `.gitignore` deliberately. |
| Engine behavior fixes conflict with stale `#390` | `backend/main.py` | Land correctness/resource fixes first, then close/re-cut the split from fresh `origin/master`. |
| UI fixes regress layout or localized text | `khelsutra/web`, `site`, extension UI | Unit/e2e coverage plus Playwright screenshots/screencast validation. |
| Local cleanup looks durable but is machine-only | untracked debris / `.git` gc | Record local-only as local-only; add durable ignore/cleanup tests only when needed. |
| Owner-gated work gets blended into engineering PRs | Cloudflare, corpus, legal, launch | Keep gate rows marked `Gated? Owner`; PRs may document but not perform. |

### Honest limits

A green docs-only PR proves only that the project is tracked and links are valid. It does not fix runtime debt.

A green unit suite does not prove GPU, WSL, real-video, Cloudflare, Google Drive desktop, or GCS bucket behavior unless the PR explicitly runs those paths or records owner verification.

Screencasts prove rendered behavior for the captured viewport and flow; they do not replace automated tests. Any UI PR that fixes a bug must still add or update deterministic tests where feasible.

### Definition of done

- [x] All non-owner-gated P0/P1/P2/P3 audit findings either resolved, explicitly reclassified, or moved into a narrower tracked project.
- [x] Every code repo has a credible local/CI quality gate for its size: tests, lint, type checking where appropriate, and coverage floor where feasible.
- [x] Engine cost leaks and scratch leaks are fixed or guarded (P2–P7).
- [x] Engine vendored SPA bundle is current or has a durable staleness signal.
- [x] SPA processing UX survives refresh and gives testers visible job/file state.
- [x] UI-visible PRs include validated visual artifacts and deterministic tests where feasible.
- [x] Stale deployment docs/issues no longer direct future sessions at retired infrastructure.
- [x] Owner-gated items remain clearly labeled and have no accidental code/data mutation.
- [x] This tracker's rows are kept current with merged PR numbers.
- [x] When the waves complete, flip this umbrella `Status → DONE`; archival is deferred while residual owner-gated choices stay linked here.

- `2026-07-02` — **P23 step 2: server_runtime extraction (`#432`).** Moved the 7 pure server/network bootstrap helpers (CORS/host-allowlist config + the `indexer --app` launcher) → `backend/server_runtime.py` (AST-identical move; re-imported above `app = FastAPI(...)` since middleware calls two of them at import time; re-exported for the 24 `m.<name>` test call sites). `main.py` **1703 → 1623**; ratchet tightened 1750 → 1650. Full suite 1579✓/cov 85.16%, mypy clean; the suite caught 3 launcher tests patching the moved `webbrowser` attr on `backend.main` → repointed to `backend.server_runtime`.
- `2026-07-02` — **Final tracker reconciliation (`#439`).** Replaced all `this PR`/Todo/Blocked placeholders with actual merged PRs, marked P24 scope-retired per owner instruction, and moved unresolved work into the residual owner-gated/future-backlog table.
- `2026-07-02` — **P28 golden source video hashes (`sports-data-collector#60`).** Mounted `F:`, MD5-hashed the six owned source MP4s (~39 GB), updated the collector DB paths, and committed `data/golden_source_videos.json` with a regression test; upload remains owner-gated.
- `2026-07-02` — **P27 Roora pilot non-video archive (`#438`).** Reconciled the local non-video Roora pilot record into `gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/archive/roora-pilot-2026-06/`: 717 small annotation objects were already present, then 22 missing sidecars/CSV/trajectory files plus `manifest.json` were uploaded. Verification: 740 objects under the prefix, manifest schema `khelsutra.roora_pilot_archive.v1`, 739 manifest entries / 5,731,641 bytes, and **0 video objects**; raw videos remain owner-gated.
- `2026-07-02` — **P8 upload-retention wrapper (`#433`).** Added `tools.upload_retention`, a dry-run-by-default scheduled wrapper over the already-tested `tools.cleanup_caches` upload-retention planner. It supports one-shot cron/systemd/Task Scheduler use and a long-running `--interval-minutes` loop for hosted wrappers; docs now place it in the alpha go-live checklist. Focused tests cover stale-source planning, dry-run safety, apply/delete behavior, delete failures, and the timer seam.
- `2026-07-02` — **P9 trackers SHA pin (`#434`).** Resolved roboflow/trackers tag `2.5.0` to immutable commit `1bf611d95262450c04fcf093334dd4c7116a2df7`, moved the git requirement into `requirements-trackers.txt`, and made requirements, CI, nightly, Cloud Run, and DigitalOcean bootstrap consume that single source instead of hard-coded mutable tag literals.
- `2026-07-02` — **P10 obsreport pin (`#435`).** Updated the engine `reporting` extra from the stale `avidullu/sports-obsreport` SSH redirect and mutable `v0.1.3` tag to the canonical HTTPS `Khelsutra/sports-obsreport` repo pinned at commit `a0c7252c6e4159f8702360bea1cb46e5148b5c9f` (sports-obsreport `#1`: CI coverage gate + `match` plural fix).
- `2026-07-02` — **P15 vendored SPA refresh (`#436`).** Rebuilt committed `backend/ui/` from KhelSutra web commit `61e5ccb649955e2177d24818428e7b4795c46724` (after P16-P21 landed) and added a doctor-time bundled-UI freshness warning against a sibling `khelsutra` checkout.
- `2026-07-02` — **P25 database facade split (`#437`).** Kept `backend.infrastructure.database.Database` as the only public facade while moving media/segment CRUD, async jobs/cost/policy, and user-model store methods into domain mixins (`database_media.py`, `database_jobs.py`, `database_user_models.py`). Schema migration and connection logic remain centralized in `database.py`.
- `2026-07-02` — **P23 step 1: god-module split started (`#431`).** Closed stale `#390` (its extracted copies predated the #417/#424–#427 rewrites) and re-cut fresh: `backend/orchestration.py` (10 processing/compile orchestration members, machine-verified AST-identical modulo 7 enumerated call-time-seam deltas; `backend.main` re-exports keep every monkeypatch/worker seam working). `main.py` **2360 → 1703 lines**. Added the **anti-bloat ratchet** `tests/test_main_module_budget.py` (line budget 1750, tightened by later splits · route ceiling 18, new endpoints → `backend/api/` · re-export identity contract). `backend.orchestration` is NOT mypy-quarantined (~657 lines newly type-checked; P24 down-payment). Full suite 1579✓/cov 85.16%, mypy clean, adversarial 3-lens review pre-commit (it caught the initial missing seam routing for `VideoCompiler`/`emit_indexer_report`/`compute_video_id` — fixed + re-verified).

                        try:
                            os.remove(merged_csv_path)
                        except OSError:
                            pass

            # downloaded, so the DB reflects the real file (the indexer validates
            # resolution and keys by it).
            if video_meta is not None:

                    video_meta.resolution = probed_resolution
                if probed_fps:
                    video_meta.fps = probed_fps

            if not video_meta or not rallies:
                print("  Skipping match due to missing metadata or rallies.")
                if local_path and os.path.exists(local_path):

                    # Roll back download accounting for the now-discarded file so

                    videos_downloaded -= 1
                    total_size_downloaded_mb -= download_size_mb

Sharper failure scenario: a developer temporarily hardcodes GEMINI_API_KEY or a personal path into tracked scratch/gemini_video_debugger.py mid-debug, `git status` shows it (contrary to the dir's 'kept out of git status' contract) and `git commit -a` carries it into history — the exact leak class #314 scrubbed. Fix note: `git rm --cached scratch/*.py` keeps the files on disk and they become ignored immediately since the scratch/ rule already matches untracked paths; no .gitignore edit needed. Promoting any keeper to tools/ first is optional judgment, not required for the untrack step.
origin/master:docs/AUDIT_REPORT_khelsutra-guru_2026-07-02.md:325:- verifier reasoning: .gitignore:69-71 at HEAD e410136 contains the exact 'never meant to be committed' comment and the `scratch/` rule at line 71 (verified via sed and `git check-ignore -v scratch/definitely_new_file.py` → .gitignore:71). `git ls-files scratch` returns exactly the 7 claimed files (scratch/analyze_motion.py, clean_state.py, diagnose_rally.py, gemini_video_debugger.py, list_models.py, test_ai_segmenter.py, test_thresholds.py). History confirms tracking since 72f972e (2026-05-18) plus 6b7d5dd; last scratch/ commit (8615534) and recent .gitignore commits (aa915a7, 959c630, 71a8241/#377) did not untrack them — not fixed. Grep of tracked scratch files shows only env-var key reads (e.g. scratch/gemini_video_debugger.py:32 `os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY")`), no literal secrets, matching the finder. The trap is real: ignore rules don't apply to tracked files, so edits to these 7 appear in git status and can ride into commits despite the dir's contract; repo has proven leak-sensitivity (#314 scrub, .leakscan_private at .gitignore:76).

### 118. user (2026-07-02T16:06:32.848Z)

1	"""B6: the crawler's per-set and merged CSV temp files must be cleaned up even when
2	a download or parse fails mid-loop, so data/temp doesn't accumulate orphaned files.
3	
4	The cleanup logic lives inline in ShuttleSetCrawler.fetch_and_curate (a large method
5	that does live network I/O), so we test the CONTRACT directly: monkeypatch
6	urllib.request.urlretrieve to (a) write a real set CSV then (b) raise on the second
7	set, and assert no per-set temp file is left behind. The merged-CSV cleanup is
8	covered by the parse-failure path.
9	"""
10	from unittest import mock
11	
12	
13	def test_per_set_temp_file_cleaned_up_on_download_failure(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
14	    """B6: if urlretrieve fails for set 2 after set 1 succeeded, the set-1 temp file
15	    must still be removed (the finally block fires regardless of success/failure)."""
16	    import src.crawlers.badminton_crawler as bc
17	
18	    # Point data/temp at tmp_path so we can inspect it without touching the real tree.
19	    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
20	    (tmp_path / "data" / "temp").mkdir(parents=True)
21	    (tmp_path / "data" / "videos").mkdir(parents=True)
22	
23	    crawler = bc.ShuttleSetCrawler.__new__(bc.ShuttleSetCrawler)
24	    # Minimal attrs the inline code reads before reaching the CSV loop.
25	    crawler.auto_download = False
26	    crawler.local_dir = str(tmp_path / "data" / "videos")
27	    crawler.max_height = None
28	    crawler.max_download_size_mb = None
29	    crawler.limit_max_video_count = 1
30	    crawler.limit_max_total_size_mb = 5000

- `2026-07-02` — **Roora pilot golden-label fixtures (`sports-data-collector#56`).** Git-versioned the small human-corrected badminton, pickleball, and table-tennis Roora goldens with tests; the full non-video archive remains in GCS under `archive/roora-pilot-2026-06/`, and videos remain owner-gated.
- `2026-07-02` — **Retired-droplet docs final sweep (`khelsutra#150`).** Re-cut the stale/conflicting `khelsutra#141` from current master, removed remaining live-droplet instructions, recorded the single-origin default, and kept Cloudflare/SSH-key cleanup as owner tasks. Local verification: site tests, web typecheck, web coverage gate, and web Playwright CUJs; CI green.
- `2026-07-02` — **Final tracker reconciliation (`#439`).** Replaced all `this PR`/Todo/Blocked placeholders with actual merged PRs, marked P24 scope-retired per owner instruction, and moved unresolved work into the residual owner-gated/future-backlog table.
- `2026-07-02` — **P28 golden source video hashes (`sports-data-collector#60`).** Mounted `F:`, MD5-hashed the six owned source MP4s (~39 GB), updated the collector DB paths, and committed `data/golden_source_videos.json` with a regression test; upload remains owner-gated.
- `2026-07-02` — **P27 Roora pilot non-video archive (`#438`).** Reconciled the local non-video Roora pilot record into `gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/archive/roora-pilot-2026-06/`: 717 small annotation objects were already present, then 22 missing sidecars/CSV/trajectory files plus `manifest.json` were uploaded. Verification: 740 objects under the prefix, manifest schema `khelsutra.roora_pilot_archive.v1`, 739 manifest entries / 5,731,641 bytes, and **0 video objects**; raw videos remain owner-gated.
- `2026-07-02` — **P8 upload-retention wrapper (`#433`).** Added `tools.upload_retention`, a dry-run-by-default scheduled wrapper over the already-tested `tools.cleanup_caches` upload-retention planner. It supports one-shot cron/systemd/Task Scheduler use and a long-running `--interval-minutes` loop for hosted wrappers; docs now place it in the alpha go-live checklist. Focused tests cover stale-source planning, dry-run safety, apply/delete behavior, delete failures, and the timer seam.
- `2026-07-02` — **P9 trackers SHA pin (`#434`).** Resolved roboflow/trackers tag `2.5.0` to immutable commit `1bf611d95262450c04fcf093334dd4c7116a2df7`, moved the git requirement into `requirements-trackers.txt`, and made requirements, CI, nightly, Cloud Run, and DigitalOcean bootstrap consume that single source instead of hard-coded mutable tag literals.
- `2026-07-02` — **P10 obsreport pin (`#435`).** Updated the engine `reporting` extra from the stale `avidullu/sports-obsreport` SSH redirect and mutable `v0.1.3` tag to the canonical HTTPS `Khelsutra/sports-obsreport` repo pinned at commit `a0c7252c6e4159f8702360bea1cb46e5148b5c9f` (sports-obsreport `#1`: CI coverage gate + `match` plural fix).
- `2026-07-02` — **P15 vendored SPA refresh (`#436`).** Rebuilt committed `backend/ui/` from KhelSutra web commit `61e5ccb649955e2177d24818428e7b4795c46724` (after P16-P21 landed) and added a doctor-time bundled-UI freshness warning against a sibling `khelsutra` checkout.
- `2026-07-02` — **P25 database facade split (`#437`).** Kept `backend.infrastructure.database.Database` as the only public facade while moving media/segment CRUD, async jobs/cost/policy, and user-model store methods into domain mixins (`database_media.py`, `database_jobs.py`, `database_user_models.py`). Schema migration and connection logic remain centralized in `database.py`.
- `2026-07-02` — **P23 step 1: god-module split started (`#431`).** Closed stale `#390` (its extracted copies predated the #417/#424–#427 rewrites) and re-cut fresh: `backend/orchestration.py` (10 processing/compile orchestration members, machine-verified AST-identical modulo 7 enumerated call-time-seam deltas; `backend.main` re-exports keep every monkeypatch/worker seam working). `main.py` **2360 → 1703 lines**. Added the **anti-bloat ratchet** `tests/test_main_module_budget.py` (line budget 1750, tightened by later splits · route ceiling 18, new endpoints → `backend/api/` · re-export identity contract). `backend.orchestration` is NOT mypy-quarantined (~657 lines newly type-checked; P24 down-payment). Full suite 1579✓/cov 85.16%, mypy clean, adversarial 3-lens review pre-commit (it caught the initial missing seam routing for `VideoCompiler`/`emit_indexer_report`/`compute_video_id` — fixed + re-verified).
